**AI pay right now (12 Nov 2026):** the US median posted pay for AI & machine learning roles is **$218k** a year (middle half $180k to $260k, from 2,584 job posts). 122 of the 300 open roles below pay at least 10% above the median for their country and level. The highest posted ranges in the US are at Anthropic, General Intuition, Thinking Machines Lab, Hark, Micron and Intuitive.

**Legend:** 🔥 top-rated company · 🟧 YC-backed · 🌐 remote · 🟢 pays 10%+ above the market median for the same country, role and level. Posted pay is the yearly range in the job post, in its own currency; ≈ shows US dollars. Apply opens the landed job page, which links to the employer's own application.

## Common questions

### How much do AI engineers make?

In the US, posted pay for AI & machine learning roles has a median of $218k a year, with the middle half between $180k and $260k. Pay rises with level: see [Market pay](#market-pay) for entry to director.

### Which companies pay the most for AI roles?

Among open US roles with posted pay, the highest ranges right now are at Anthropic, General Intuition, Thinking Machines Lab, Hark, Micron and Intuitive.

### Where do these numbers come from?

From pay ranges employers publish in their own job posts (many US states and EU countries require them). The market tables cover every live job post with yearly pay in landed's job database, converted to US dollars. Nothing here is self-reported.

### How do I apply to the roles that pay above the median?

[landed](https://app.landed.jobs/signup?from=github&cta=github-list-bulk&item=ai-salaries&utm_source=github&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=github-ai-salaries&utm_content=faq) matches the roles here to your resume and applies for you, and Enzo drafts your answers for you to review.

---

<details>
<summary><b>How this list is built</b></summary>

This list is generated from landed's live job database and refreshed every two days. It keeps open AI & machine learning and research roles posted in the last 90 days whose job post states yearly pay and links to an employer application. US roles come first. Within each section, companies are ordered by their highest posted pay, at most 3 roles each, and staffing agencies are left out.

Market pay is computed from every live job post with yearly pay, grouped by country, role family and level, using the midpoint of each posted range converted to US dollars. A cell needs at least 10 posts to be shown. "vs market median" compares a role's midpoint to the median for its country, family and level (or its country and family when the level has too few posts).

Spotted a wrong range or a closed role? [Open an issue](https://github.com/landedjobs/ai-salaries/issues/new).

</details>
